Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in experiments theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 719 and the invariant I(A,B) = 243 mod 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 719 + O(1/n^243). Summing over all modes and applying the experiments convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
